R9647_en.md

# R9647, Venice, 7 Nov 1522 — English summary of the reading (Brice Dudout, 12 Nov 2026, C04)

A summary, not a translation, of `R9647_text_es.txt` (read in part, 75.6 % strict; unresolved words left out). Brackets
mark what the unresolved words leave uncertain. The clear date at the foot of f. 258v reads *a vij de noviembre*
(DECODE: 6 Nov).

**f. 257r.** The Signoria receives letters from France often; a few days ago some came by way of [...], and those of
the French party hold back their letters so that what they are doing is not known. Their news: French affairs are
going [well]; the army of His Majesty and of [the Pope?] withdrew without doing anything; the French will come this
[winter] to the enterprise of [Milan?]; the King of France [is coming?] and must pay his men the money promised them
by Candlemas, so that the enterprise is to be made at once. Sánchez knows nothing certain, but the Republic may be
[helping] the French with money. The French ambassador goes on [frequenting] the Signoria. Yesterday he and the English
ambassador went to the Signoria and told them that what they had to say was as persons wishing its good and repose,
and as an office of ambassadors, each having been advised by letters from outside; the French partisans here are
plotting with them.

**f. 257v.** If it were true [that they had made] an agreement with the King of France against His Majesty — as they
had often said they had not — it would break the peace. They answered that they now had no obligation nor capitulation
with the King of France, being released from it, and that anything done would be against the truce they have with His
Majesty; it was not true, they wished only to live in peace. They complained strongly of the answer given in England,
and of the detention of their [ships/goods] in His Majesty's lands, saying they did not know why England did so.
Sánchez answered that His Majesty's will towards them was very good, if they gave no cause for the contrary; the
English ambassador would satisfy them about England. They said they still [...] fifty thousand ducats of damage.

**f. 258r.** Sánchez thinks they [...] in their obligation to France; they did not answer him. The English ambassador
told him they had said they had no more obligation to the French, but Sánchez does not believe they will [refrain from]
dealing with the King of France: he knows them French at heart, and they are very secret with the French ambassador,
seeking money by every way. He has written to His Majesty, to don Juan Manuel and to the Duke of [Sessa]. The Republic
wrote to its nuncio [in Rome?] on the 16th of last month to work for the universal peace among Christian princes, and
sent [word] to the nuncio about another [document] of the [24th].

**f. 258v.** The English ambassador [acted] gladly. Sánchez learned of another [paper] of the 24th sent to the
Signoria, not to them; [the Pope?] is said to have received their letters. Of Geronimo Adorno he knows nothing since
his letter of the 28th of last month. When the English ambassador asked the Signoria to declare against France, he was
told they could not excuse themselves [from] the [league] with [the Pope]: that confederation was with [the Pope] and
the Apostolic See, the principal party, [...].

## Unknown code groups (not in key_codes.tsv; proposals only, none applied — PREREG_lot3 measure FAILED)
- **dud** (×2): 257r.11 "el Rey de Francia <dud> para este [...]" — *vino / viene* fits (C03's "vino"); 257r.15 "me ha
  dicho que <dud> [...]" — again a verb (*vino*, *venia*?). Context does not decide.
- **hod** (×4): "el exercito de vuestra magestad y del <hod>", "en periuyzio de vuestra magestad y del <hod>",
  "que el <hod>": a party allied with the Emperor — *papa* (the Pope) or *duque* of Milan.
- **mif** (×5): "el <mif>", "la <nab> del <mif>", "confederacion ... fue con el <mif> y con la sede apostolica": *papa*.
- **pem** (×4), **him** (×2, "por via de <him>", "la <him>a don joan manuel" — *Roma*?), **hag** (×3), **yec** (×3,
  "otro <yec> de xxiv" — *breve* / *escrito*?), **nid** (×3, "<nid>cion" — *negociacion*?), **feg** (×2), **ref**
  (×2, "la enpresa de <ref>" — *Milan*?), **nof** (×2), **hac** (×2), **ni**, **pod**, **vef**, **cub**, **gog**,
  **guc**, **reg**, **pab**, **zih**, **nig**, **go**, **tim**, **bic**, **na**, **val**, **nab**, **nol**, **pum**, **tal**.
